Let's consider optimizing using the two-block reversed orientation parameterization:
- Choose length n = 3m (m=n//3) if divisible, or general.
- Sequence: u (length m) + zeros(gap) + R(v) maybe with v chosen to match u? Actually to make all pulses same autocorrelation, set right block = reverse(u) maybe if left u, right R u, then cross conv = conv(u,u). Self right = conv(Ru,Ru)=conv(u,u). Good.
- Sum = 2*sum(u). Scale invariant; set sum(u)=0.5 or 1.
- Objective = 2n * p^2 * P(u) (if p=1 maybe scale). If u normalized sum1, p=0.5, F = 2n*0.25*P = n/2 P. For general gap maybe n/(2m)? But shape minimization.
- For uniform u, P=1/m, F=n/(2m)=1.5 if n=3m.
- We can optimize u to minimize max conv(u,u) subject sum1, u>=0. This is a 1602-dimensional minimax nonnegative problem. But maybe uniform optimal; search could verify/possibly improve.

How to optimize P(u) efficiently? We can use Frank-Wolfe or subgradient. But likely no improvement. Still, we can include limited search to be safe.

Alternative: Use direct global search for two-block shapes with separable patterns: e.g., u from a small parametrized family (linear ramp, cosine, edge-peaked) to reduce P. Algorithm details:
- Work with normalized sum=1 arrays `a`.
- Objective F = 2*n*M. We can evaluate with evaluate_sequence on scaled list to be safe. But in loop, compute M = max(np.convolve(a,a)), score=2*n*M (since sum=1). Need ensure a length n; if zeros etc.
- Projected gradient:
  For current a, c = conv(a,a). M=max(c). Use `peaks = c >= max(M - eps, 0.999*M)` maybe to approximate subgradient; or use logsumexp weights for smoothness.
  Subgradient of max using set of peaks (within tolerance) is stable. We can compute g_i = mean over peaks of (a[k-i]+a[i-k]). Use direct loops over peaks if few; for uniform many peaks? Uniform c triangular, only center peak one. For high-frequency, many peaks. Could use smooth weights: w = softmax(β c / M?) to avoid overflow.
  Projected gradient step: a_new = project_simplex(a - step * g)
  But objective scale fixed sum1; g has dimensions. Step size? M ~1/n. g ~ M? Since each a~1/n, g_i ~ sum w a ~1/n. Step size maybe O(n) to move. Use adaptive: step = c0 * M perhaps? Let's analyze. We want change a by O(1/n). g ~1/n. If step=1, change ~1/n. Good. Use step = maybe 1 or 2. But projected gradient on max can oscillate. Use learning rate schedule: start 1.0, reduce if not improving.
  Alternatively, use Frank-Wolfe: direction v = projection? For minimizing max, the steepest descent on simplex can be found by minimizing linearization ∑ g_i v_i over simplex: choose v one-hot at index with min g_i, then a_new = (1-t)a + t v. This is efficient and respects sum1. Step t maybe. This is conditional gradient (Frank-Wolfe). The linearization of M at a is L(z)=M+g·(z-a). Minimizing over simplex: if g has negative components, choose index with most negative g. But g_i = derivative of max c; for max coefficient, g_i likely positive? For nonconvex, subgradient not sufficient. Need use smooth objective or consider multiple peaks? Max gradient constant implies constant local? Coordinate descent idea: Since objective is max diagonal sum, we can improve by moving probability mass from coordinates that contribute to max diagonals to those that contribute to low diagonals. We can implement a "mass shifting" heuristic:
- Maintain normalized a.
- Find lag k with maximum c_k.
- For each i, the amount of a_i contributing to c_k through pair with a_{k-i}. To reduce c_k, reduce a_i for indices where partner contributes large. To not increase other max, choose donors and recipients.
- A simple operation: pick two indices i,j. Move small mass δ from i to j. Compute change in max approximately using current c and local pairs. Accept if improves. This is like 2-opt. For n=4806, O(n^2) pairs impossible, but we can sample.

Let's formulate recurrence as a function of q list:
```
def sequence_from_profile(q, a0):
    a=np.zeros(n); a[0]=a0
    for k in range(1,n):
        known = sum_{i=1}^{k-1} a[i]*a[k-i]
        val=(q[k] - known)/(2*a0)
        if val<0: fail
        a[k]=val
    return a
```
We can compute known efficiently using convolution prefix? For one sequence, O(n^2). For optimization many q, O(n^2*iterations) too much if n=4806. But we can first optimize on small n (e.g., 256) and find profile shape, then evaluate large n. Since recurrence cost O(n^2) for n=256 trivial. Then apply profile to large n once.

```
def random_sequence(n, rng): return rng.random(n)+1e-6 normalized
def local_search_small(n, budget):
  best=None
  for init in [uniform, hard-core, random, ramp, sin]:
    a=normalize(init)
    score=fast_score(a)
    for steps:
      # block perturbation: choose two blocks? random mask
      cand = a * exp(eps * random block pattern)
      cand=clip, normalize
      if score better...
  return best
```
For n=64, evaluating many thousands possible. Use `np.convolve` length127 cheap.

- For each base (small shape upscaled, hard-core, uniform, sine, ramp), test `tilt` and `power` via grid and local golden.

import numpy as np
import time

def normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a,float)
    a = np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    return a/s

def fast_score(a):
    # assumes a normalized sum1 or can normalize
    if a is None: return inf
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return inf
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return inf
    a=a/s
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    M=np.max(c)
    return 2*a.size*M

def to_eval_list(a):
    a=np.clip(np.asarray(a,float),0,None)
    if a.max()<=0: return [1.0]
    scale=1000.0/a.max()
    # if sum*scale too low? a sum1 -> scale>=1? if max>1, scale<1 maybe sum<1? But evaluate sum needs >0.01; if a max small e.g. 1e-3, scale large >1000? Actually max(a) for normalized length n ~1/√n ~0.01, scale 100000 >? Values scaled max 1000, sum=100000>0.01. Good. If a max >1, scale<1 and sum may <0.01? But normalized sum1, max cannot exceed1. So scale≥1. Fine.
    b=a*scale
    b=np.clip(b,0,1000)
    return b.tolist()

def seq_hard_core(n):
   a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0; for i in range(1,n): a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)?? Check recurrence: a_i = central binom/4^i. a_{i}=a_{i-1}*(2i-1)/(2i). For i=1:1/2, yes (1/2). Loop i=1..n-1.
   return a
```

Function `smooth_gradient_simplex(a, beta)`:
```
c=np.convolve(a,a)
shift=np.max(c/beta)
ew=np.exp(c/beta - shift)
w=ew/ew.sum()
# compute g = term1 + term2
# For small m, loop over i with vector dot? m=128 ->16k ops per gradient, okay.
n=len(a)
g=np.zeros(n)
for i in range(n):
    # term1: a[k-i] k where valid
    kmin=max(0,i)?? k from 0..2n-2. For valid k-i in [0,n-1] -> k in [i, i+n-1]
    if i+n-1 <= 2n-2: k1=i; k2=min(2n-2,i+n-1)
    idx = np.arange(k1,k2+1)
    s1 = np.dot(w[idx], a[idx-i]) # idx-i =0..k2-i
    # term2: a[i-k] valid k in [0,i]
    if i>=0: k1=0; k2=min(2n-2,i)
    idx2=np.arange(0,k2+1)
    s2=np.dot(w[idx2], a[i-idx2])
    g[i]=s1+s2
return g
```
But for m=128, loops 128 * dot lengths ~8k, fine. For m=512, loops 512*~1k=500k, okay.

def project_simplex(v, s=1.0): ...

  g=smooth_gradient_simplex(a,beta)
  v=a - step*g
  new=project_simplex(v)
  return new
```

Upsampling small shape:
```
def upsample(n, shape):
   a=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), np.linspace(0,1,len(shape), endpoint=True), shape)
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   return normalize(a)
```
If shape has endpoints high, interpolation preserves roughly.

def random_block_candidate(a, rng):
  n=len(a); cand=a.copy(); width=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//200), max(3,n//10))); start=...
  cand[start:stop]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.2))
  if rng.random()<0.2: # scale tail vs head
     split=...; factor=exp(noise); cand[:split]*=factor; cand[split:]*=1/factor
  return normalize(cand)

Small_n search details:
```
def small_n_search(budget_s, rng):
  shapes=[]
  deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
  for m in (32,64):
    inits=[]
    inits.append(normalize(np.ones(m)))
    inits.append(normalize(make_hardcore(m)))
    inits.append(normalize(np.linspace(0,1,m)))
    inits.append(normalize(1.0/(np.linspace(0,1,m)+0.01)))
    inits.append(normalize(np.abs(np.sin(np.arange(m)*0.7))))
    inits.append(normalize(rng.random(m)+0.1))
    for a0 in inits:
       a=project_simplex(a0.copy())
       score=fast_score(a)
       step=1.0
       for it in range(2000):
          if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
          beta = max(1e-12, np.max(np.convolve(a,a))/50)  # temp
          g = smooth_gradient_simplex(a, beta)
          cand = project_simplex(a - step*g)
          cs = fast_score(cand)
          if cs < score - 1e-12:
             a=cand; score=cs; step*=1.03
          else:
             step*=0.75
             if step < 1e-8:
                # random jump
                a = project_simplex(a * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.3,size=m)))
                step=1.0
                score=fast_score(a)
       if score < 2.0:
          shapes.append(a)
          # store unique best
```

`project_simplex` with s=1.0. Implementation careful with empty:
```
def project_simplex(v, s=1.0):
    n=len(v)
    if n==0: return v
    if s<=0: return np.zeros(n)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.where(u*np.arange(1,n+1) > cssv - s)[0]
    if len(rho)==0:
       return np.full(n, s/n)
    rho=rho[-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-s)/(rho+1.0)
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    # fix rounding
    total=w.sum()
    if total>0: w *= s/total
    else: w=np.full(n,s/n)
    return w
```

`smooth_gradient_simplex(a, tau)`:
```
c=np.convolve(a,a)
z=c/tau
maxz=np.max(z)
ew=np.exp(z-maxz)
w=ew/ew.sum()
n=len(a)
g=np.zeros(n)
full_len=2*n-1
for i in range(n):
   k_start=max(0,i)
   k_end=min(full_len-1,i+n-1)
   if k_start<=k_end:
      idx=np.arange(k_start,k_end+1)
      g[i]+=np.dot(w[idx], a[idx-i])
   k_start2=0
   k_end2=min(full_len-1,i)
   if k_start2<=k_end2:
      idx2=np.arange(k_start2,k_end2+1)
      g[i]+=np.dot(w[idx2], a[i-idx2])
return g
```
Check term indices: For term1 ∑_k w_k a_{k-i}, valid k-i ∈[0,n-1] -> k∈[i, i+n-1]. idx-i range [0,k_end-i]. Good. Term2 ∑_k w_k a_{i-k}, valid i-k ∈[0,n-1] -> k∈[0,i]. idx2 range [0,i], a[i-idx2]. Good.

`block_perturb`:
```
def make_block_candidate(a, rng):
  n=len(a); cand=a.copy()
  if rng.random()<0.25:
    # global tilt
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    theta=rng.normal(0,0.2)
    cand = a * np.exp(theta*t)
  else:
    width=int(rng.integers(max(2, n//500), max(3, n//8)))
    start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width)))
    cand[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.3)))
    if rng.random()<0.2:
       cand = 0.5*(cand+cand[::-1]) # but may break asymmetry
  return normalize_positive(cand)
```

Let's implement `local_search(arr, best_score, deadline, rng, max_iter=None)` top-level:
```
def local_search(a, best_score, deadline, rng, max_steps=5000):
   a=normalize_positive(a)
   score=fast_score(a)
   step_noise=0.25
   stall=0
   for it in range(max_steps):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      for _ in range(1,4):
         cand=make_block_candidate(a, rng)
         if cand is None: continue
         s=fast_score(cand)
         if s < best_score:
             best_score=s; best=cand
         if s < score:
             score=s; a=cand; stall=0
             step_noise=min(0.5, step_noise*1.02)
         else:
             stall+=1
      if stall>20:
         step_noise=max(0.05, step_noise*0.7)
         a = make_block_candidate(a, rng) # random jump?
         score=fast_score(a)
   return a, best_score
```

Small local search:
```
def small_local_step(a, rng):
  n=len(a); cand=a.copy()
  if rng.random()<0.2:
    theta=rng.normal(0,0.3)
    cand=a*np.exp(theta*np.linspace(0,1,n))
  else:
    width=int(rng.integers(1,max(2,n//4)))
    start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width))
    cand[start:start+width]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5))
  return normalize_positive(cand)
```

def make_profile_sequence(n, A, B):
    q=np.ones(n)
    if B!=0: q = 1 - A*(np.arange(n)/(n-1))**B
    # maybe q[0]=1
    a=np.zeros(n); a0=np.sqrt(max(1e-12,q[0])); a[0]=a0
    # known via convolution increment? O(n^2) loop:
    for k in range(1,n):
       # known = sum_{i=1}^{k-1} a[i]*a[k-i]
       if k>2: known=np.dot(a[1:k-1], a[k-1:0:-1])? Need align: i=1..k-1, k-i ranges k-1..1. Dot a[1:k] with reversed a[1:k]? For i=1..k-1: a[i]*a[k-i]. This is dot(a[1:k], a[k-1:0:-1])? For length k-1: a[1:k] and a[k-1:0:-1] (from k-1 down1). Use np.dot.
       else known=0
       val=(q[k]-known)/(2*a0)
       if val<0: break fail
       a[k]=val
    if fail return None
    return normalize(a)
```

def inv_sqrt_seq(n):
   return 1.0/np.sqrt(np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float))
```

a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
for i in range(1,n): a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
return a
```

`normalize_positive`:
```
def normalize_positive(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
  if a.size==0: return None
  a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
  a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
  s=a.sum()
  if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300: return None
  return a/s
```

`fast_score` handles None.

`to_eval_list`:
```
def to_eval_list(a):
  a=normalize_positive(a)
  if a is None: return [1.0]
  mx=float(a.max())
  if mx<=0: return [1.0]
  scale=1000.0/mx
  b=a*scale
  b=np.clip(b,0.0,1000.0)
  # ensure sum >0.01 after clipping? if b.sum() <0.01: scale more? But sum=1000/mx; mx cannot >1 for normalized, so >=1000. OK.
  return [float(x) for x in b]
```
If a max <1/scale? normalized max<=1. yes.

Small search function:
```
def run_small_search(budget_s, rng):
  out=[]
  t_end=time.monotonic()+budget_s
  m_list=[32,64]
  for m in m_list:
    inits=[]
    inits.append(np.ones(m))
    inits.append(make_hardcore(m))
    inits.append(inv_sqrt_seq(m))
    inits.append(np.linspace(1.0,0.1,m))
    inits.append(np.linspace(0.1,1.0,m))
    inits.append(np.abs(np.sin(np.arange(m)*0.3)))
    inits.append(np.abs(np.cos(np.arange(m)*0.2)))
    inits.append(rng.random(m)+0.1)
    # profile?
    for a0 in inits:
      a=normalize_positive(a0)
      if a is None: continue
      score=fast_score(a)
      stall=0
      noise=0.4
      for it in range(3000):
        if time.monotonic()>t_end: break
        cand=make_small_block_candidate(a,rng)
        if cand is None: continue
        cs=fast_score(cand)
        if cs < score - 1e-12:
           a=cand; score=cs; stall=0; noise=min(0.8, noise*1.02)
        else:
           stall+=1
           if stall>15:
              noise=max(0.1, noise*0.7)
              a=make_small_block_candidate(a,rng)
              score=fast_score(a)
              stall=0
      out.append((score,a))
  out.sort(key=lambda x:x[0]) # lambda forbidden! Need avoid lambda. Use out.sort(key=cmp?) We can implement sort manually or use `out.sort(key=score_key)` with top-level function `def score_key(item): return item[0]`.
  return [a for _,a in out[:6]]
```
No lambda: define top-level `score_key(item): return item[0]`.

`make_small_block_candidate(a,rng)`:
```
n=len(a); cand=a.copy(); r=rng.random()
if r<0.15:
  theta=rng.normal(0,0.25)
  t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  cand=a*np.exp(theta*t)
elif r<0.35:
  split=int(rng.integers(1,n))
  fac=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.3)))
  cand[:split]*=fac
  cand[split:]*=1/fac
else:
  width=int(rng.integers(1,max(2,n//5)))
  start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width))
  cand[start:start+width]*=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5)))
return normalize_positive(cand)
```

Large local candidate `make_large_block_candidate(a,rng, mode)` similar but wider.
```
def make_large_block_candidate(a,rng):
  n=len(a); r=rng.random(); cand=a.copy()
  if r<0.1:
     theta=rng.normal(0,0.15); t=np.linspace(0,1,n); cand=a*np.exp(theta*t)
  elif r<0.2:
     split=int(rng.integers(1,n)); fac=exp(normal(0,0.15)); cand[:split]*=fac; cand[split:]*=1/fac
  else:
     width=int(rng.integers(max(3,n//500), max(5,n//10)))
     start=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-width)))
     cand[start:start+width]*=exp(normal(0,0.25))
     if rng.random()<0.15: cand=0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])
  return normalize_positive(cand)
```

Param sweeps function:
```
def param_candidates(base, rng):
  n=len(base); t=np.linspace(0,1,n); arr=[]
  thetas=np.concatenate((np.arange(-1.0,1.0+1e-9,0.25), np.arange(-0.25,0.25+1e-9,0.05)))
  for th in thetas:
    arr.append(base*np.exp(th*t))
  alphas=np.arange(0.7,1.31,0.05)
  for al in alphas:
    arr.append(base**al)
  # polynomial tail
  for amp in (0.3,0.6,1.0):
    for p in (2,3):
       arr.append(base*(1.0+amp*t**p))
       arr.append(base*(1.0+amp*(1.0-t)**p))
  # cosine mod
  for freq in (1,2,3,5,8):
    for amp in (0.1,0.25,0.4):
       for phase in (0.0,1.57079632679):
          factor=1.0+amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*freq*t+phase)
          if factor.min()>1e-6:
             arr.append(base*factor)
  return arr
```
This can generate many (13+13+8*? +15 ~80). For each base. We can limit bases to e.g. 6 to keep time.

Upsampling:
```
def upsample(shape,n):
  x1=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,len(shape),endpoint=True)
  x2=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=True)
  a=np.interp(x2,x1,shape)
  return normalize_positive(a)
```

Profile sequence small search? Add function `make_profile(n,A,p)` and include in small candidate pool. For small m, O(m^2) fine.
```
def make_profile_sequence(n,A,p):
  q=np.ones(n)
  x=np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1)
  q = 1.0 - A*(x**p)
  q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
  a=np.zeros(n); a0=np.sqrt(max(1e-12,q[0])); a[0]=a0
  for k in range(1,n):
    known=0.0
    if k>2:
       # dot a[1:k] with a[k-1:0:-1]
       known=float(np.dot(a[1:k], a[k-1:0:-1]))
    val=(q[k]-known)/(2*a0)
    if val<0:
       # try reducing? return None
       return None
    a[k]=val
  return normalize_positive(a)
```
Check dot for k=3: a[1:3] length2 [a1,a2]; a[2:0:-1] -> indices2,1 length2; dot a1*a2+a2*a1=2a1a2. Good.
Include in small inits for A=0.1,0.3,0.5, p=1,2.

But profile with q decreasing might fail positivity for A too large. If None skip.

Now propose flow:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(60,budget_s)-45
  n_list=[1024,2048,4096,4806,6144]
  best_score=np.inf; best_arr=None
  # include height_sequence_1 normalized?
  try:
    hs=normalize_positive(np.asarray(height_sequence_1,dtype=float))
    if hs is not None:
      s=fast_score(hs)
      if s<best_score: ...
  except Exception: pass
  # hardcore and invsqrt for n_list
  for n in n_list:
    for base in [make_hardcore(n), inv_sqrt_seq(n)]:
      a=normalize_positive(base)
      s=fast_score(a)
      if s<best_score: update
  # param sweeps on top bases: collect up to 6 best arrays from initial pool
  pool=[] append when considering? We need maintain list. We'll have top-level `add_to_pool`? Simpler in propose: create `pool_scores=[]` list of (score,arr) top-level key for sort.

```python
"""
Search for low-scoring non-negative step functions.

The program combines:
  * explicit asymptotically strong bases (hard-core 1/sqrt-like sequences),
  * small-length stochastic shape searches,
  * interpolation/up-sampling of good shapes,
  * structured parametric sweeps (tilt, power, cosine, polynomial end boosts),
  * large-length stochastic local refinement.

The returned sequence is always non-negative, finite, and scaled so no value
is clipped by the evaluator.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def score_item(item):
    return item[0]


def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def fast_score(a):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def project_simplex(v, s=1.0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.where(u * np.arange(1, n + 1) > (cssv - s))[0]
    if rho.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, s / n, dtype=float)
    r = int(rho[-1])
    theta = (cssv[r] - s) / float(r + 1)
    w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    total = float(np.sum(w))
    if total > 0.0 and np.isfinite(total):
        w *= s / total
    else:
        w = np.full(n, s / n, dtype=float)
    return w


def make_hardcore(n):
    a = np.empty(int(n), dtype=float)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * float(2 * i - 1) / float(2 * i)
    return a


def make_inv_sqrt(n):
    return 1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=float))


def make_profile_sequence(n, amp, power):
    n = int(n)
    q = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        x = np.arange(n, dtype=float) / float(n - 1)
        q = 1.0 - amp * (x ** power)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)

    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    a0 = float(np.sqrt(max(q[0], 1e-12)))
    if a0 <= 0.0:
        return None
    a[0] = a0

    for k in range(1, n):
        known = 0.0
        if k > 2:
            known = float(np.dot(a[1:k], a[k - 1:0:-1]))
        val = (float(q[k]) - known) / (2.0 * a0)
        if not np.isfinite(val) or val < 0.0:
            return None
        a[k] = val
    return normalize_positive(a)


def upsample_interp(shape, n):
    shape = normalize_positive(shape)
    if shape is None:
        return None
    m = int(shape.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return None
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, endpoint=True)
    x2 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n), endpoint=True)
    return normalize_positive(np.interp(x2, x1, shape))


def upsample_repeat(shape, n):
    shape = normalize_positive(shape)
    if shape is None:
        return None
    m = int(shape.size)
    n = int(n)
    if m <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return None
    rep = int(np.ceil(float(n) / float(m)))
    a = np.tile(shape, rep)[:n]
    return normalize_positive(a)


def param_candidates(base):
    base = normalize_positive(base)
    if base is None:
        return []
    n = int(base.size)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=True)
    out = []

    thetas = np.concatenate(
        (
            np.arange(-1.0, 1.0 + 1e-9, 0.25),
            np.arange(-0.25, 0.25 + 1e-9, 0.05),
        )
    )
    for th in thetas:
        out.append(base * np.exp(float(th) * t))

    alphas = np.arange(0.70, 1.31, 0.05)
    for al in alphas:
        out.append(np.power(np.maximum(base, 1e-12), float(al)))

    for amp in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
        for p in (2, 3):
            out.append(base * (1.0 + amp * (t ** p)))
            out.append(base * (1.0 + amp * ((1.0 - t) ** p)))

    for freq in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8):
        for amp in (0.10, 0.25, 0.40):
            for phase in (0.0, 1.5707963267948966):
                factor = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * t + phase)
                if factor.min() > 1e-6:
                    out.append(base * factor)

    cleaned = []
    for a in out:
        b = normalize_positive(a)
        if b is not None and b.size == n:
            cleaned.append(b)
    return cleaned


def make_small_block_candidate(a, rng):
    n = int(a.size)
    r = float(rng.random())
    cand = a.copy()
    if r < 0.15:
        theta = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25))
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        cand = a * np.exp(theta * t)
    elif r < 0.35:
        split = int(rng.integers(1, n))
        fac = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.30)))
        cand[:split] *= fac
        cand[split:] *= 1.0 / max(fac, 1e-12)
    else:
        width = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 5)))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width)))
        cand[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.50)))
    return normalize_positive(cand)


def make_large_block_candidate(a, rng):
    n = int(a.size)
    r = float(rng.random())
    cand = a.copy()
    if r < 0.10:
        theta = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.15))
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        cand = a * np.exp(theta * t)
    elif r < 0.22:
        split = int(rng.integers(1, n))
        fac = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.15)))
        cand[:split] *= fac
        cand[split:] *= 1.0 / max(fac, 1e-12)
    else:
        lo = max(3, n // 500)
        hi = max(5, n // 10)
        if hi <= lo:
            hi = lo + 1
        width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width)))
        cand[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25)))
        if rng.random() < 0.12:
            cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])
    return normalize_positive(cand)


def run_small_search(budget_s, rng):
    budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    end = time.monotonic() + budget_s
    pool = []

    m_list = [32, 64, 96]
    for m in m_list:
        inits = []
        inits.append(np.ones(m))
        inits.append(make_hardcore(m))
        inits.append(make_inv_sqrt(m))
        inits.append(np.linspace(1.0, 0.05, m))
        inits.append(np.linspace(0.05, 1.0, m))
        inits.append(np.abs(np.sin(np.arange(m) * 0.33)))
        inits.append(np.abs(np.cos(np.arange(m) * 0.21)))
        inits.append(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, m + 1) * 4.0 + 1.0))
        inits.append(np.abs(rng.normal(0, 1, size=m)) + 0.1)

        for amp in (0.1, 0.3, 0.5):
            for pw in (1.0, 2.0):
                p = make_profile_sequence(m, amp, pw)
                if p is not None:
                    inits.append(p)

        for base0 in inits:
            a = normalize_positive(base0)
            if a is None:
                continue
            score = fast_score(a)
            stall = 0
            noise = 0.45

            for _ in range(3500):
                if time.monotonic() > end:
                    break
                cand = make_small_block_candidate(a, rng)
                if cand is None:
                    continue
                cs = fast_score(cand)
                if cs < score - 1e-12:
                    a = cand
                    score = cs
                    stall = 0
                    noise = min(0.85, noise * 1.02)
                else:
                    stall += 1
                    if stall > 14:
                        noise = max(0.08, noise * 0.7)
                        cand2 = make_small_block_candidate(a, rng)
                        if cand2 is not None:
                            a = cand2
                            score = fast_score(a)
                        stall = 0

            pool.append((score, a))

    pool.sort(key=score_item)
    out = []
    last = None
    for _, a in pool:
        sig = tuple(np.round(a, 10).tolist())
        if last is None or len(out) < 8 or sig != last:
            out.append(a)
            last = sig
        if len(out) >= 8:
            break
    return out


def local_search(a, initial_best_score, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, initial_best_score
    n = int(a.size)
    score = fast_score(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = min(initial_best_score, score)
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # Try a small batch of perturbations per step.
        for _ in range(3):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            cand = make_large_block_candidate(a, rng)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            if cand.size != n:
                continue
            cs = fast_score(cand)
            if cs < best_score:
                best_score = cs
                best = cand.copy()
            if cs < score - 1e-12:
                a = cand
                score = cs
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1
        if stall > 25:
            cand = make_large_block_candidate(a, rng)
            if cand is not None and cand.size == n:
                a = cand
                score = fast_score(a)
                if score < best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best = a.copy()
            stall = 0

    return best, best_score


def to_eval_list(a):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    if a is None:
        return [1.0]
    mx = float(np.max(a))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return [1.0]
    scale = 1000.0 / mx
    b = a * scale
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in b]


def evaluated_score(seq):
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 45.0

    best_arr = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    pool = []

    def consider(a):
        nonlocal best_arr, best_score
        b = normalize_positive(a)
        if b is None:
            return
        s = fast_score(b)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            pool.append((s, b))
            if s < best_score:
                best_score = s
                best_arr = b

    # Existing global construction, if present.
    try:
        hs = normalize_positive(np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float))
        consider(hs)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Strong explicit bases at several lengths.
    lengths = [1024, 2048, 4096, 4806, 6144]
    for n in lengths:
        consider(make_hardcore(n))
        consider(make_inv_sqrt(n))

    # Parametric sweeps on current best bases.
    if pool:
        pool.sort(key=score_item)
        # Keep a small diverse set of bases.
        bases = [a for _, a in pool[:6]]
    else:
        bases = [normalize_positive(make_hardcore(4806))]

    for base in bases:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 420.0:
            break
        for cand in param_candidates(base):
            consider(cand)
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 300.0:
                break

    # Small-length stochastic search, then upsample.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 300.0:
        small_budget = min(120.0, max(0.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) - 260.0))
        if small_budget > 10.0:
            small_shapes = run_small_search(small_budget, rng)
            for shp in small_shapes:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 180.0:
                    break
                for n in (2048, 4096, 4806, 6144):
                    consider(upsample_interp(shp, n))
                    consider(upsample_repeat(shp, n))

    # Final local refinement from the strongest starts.
    if best_arr is None and pool:
        pool.sort(key=score_item)
        best_arr, best_score = pool[0]
    elif best_arr is None:
        best_arr = normalize_positive(make_hardcore(4806))
        best_score = fast_score(best_arr)

    if pool:
        pool.sort(key=score_item)
        starts = [a for _, a in pool[:3]]
        # Ensure the current best is included.
        if best_arr not in starts:
            starts.append(best_arr)
    else:
        starts = [best_arr]

    deadline_local = deadline - 20.0
    if starts and time.monotonic() < deadline_local:
        per_start = max(5.0, (deadline_local - time.monotonic()) / float(max(1, len(starts))))
        for i, st in enumerate(starts):
            cur_deadline = min(deadline_local, time.monotonic() + per_start)
            arr, sc = local_search(st, best_score, cur_deadline, rng)
            if arr is not None and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_arr = arr
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline_local:
                break

    # Validate the final normalized array by calling the official evaluator.
    final_arr = best_arr
    if final_arr is None:
        final_arr = normalize_positive(make_hardcore(4806))

    final_list = to_eval_list(final_arr)
    final_official = evaluated_score(final_list)

    # Fallback safety: the hard-core sequence scaled into evaluator range.
    fb_arr = normalize_positive(make_hardcore(4806))
    fb_list = to_eval_list(fb_arr)
    fb_official = evaluated_score(fb_list)

    if not np.isfinite(final_official) or (np.isfinite(fb_official) and fb_official < final_official):
        final_list = fb_list

    # One last safeguard against invalid official evaluation.
    if not np.isfinite(evaluated_score(final_list)):
        final_list = to_eval_list(np.ones(256, dtype=float))

    print(f"final_internal_score={fast_score(final_arr):.10f}")
    print(f"final_official_score={evaluated_score(final_list):.10f}")
    print(f"final_length={len(final_list)}")
    return final_list
```
